Ash: Move tablet mode multitask menu to a client-owned widget

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to move Ash widgets off
the deprecated NATIVE_WIDGET_OWNS_WIDGET and WIDGET_OWNS_NATIVE_WIDGET
ownership modes and onto CLIENT_OWNS_WIDGET, the mode views recommends
for all widgets. With it, the code that creates a widget owns it and
decides when it is destroyed. This removes a common source of dangling
pointers and window teardown bugs.

The menu still closes its widget when it goes away. Since that can
happen while the widget handles an event, the menu controller keeps the
closed widget and frees it in a later task.

Bug: 338254375
Test: CQ

Ash: Make the Alt+Tab window list own its widget

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to move Ash widgets off
the deprecated NATIVE_WIDGET_OWNS_WIDGET and WIDGET_OWNS_NATIVE_WIDGET
ownership modes and onto CLIENT_OWNS_WIDGET, the mode views recommends
for all widgets. With it, the code that creates a widget owns it and
decides when it is destroyed. This removes a common source of dangling
pointers and window teardown bugs.

The Alt+Tab window list now owns its widget. When cycling ends, it still
closes the widget as before and then hands it to the Alt+Tab controller,
which deletes it in a later task, once its window is gone.

Bug: 338254375
Test: CQ

media/audio/cras: Skip CRAS connections during AudioManager shutdown

When CRAS is unresponsive at browser shutdown, queued stream open and
device enumeration tasks on AudioThread block for 1000ms each in
libcras_client_connect_timeout(), causing AudioManager::Shutdown() to
exceed the 30s HangWatcher threshold while joining AudioThread.

Add AudioManagerCrasBase::IsShuttingDown(), set it at the start of
AudioManagerCrasBase::Shutdown(), pass AudioManagerCrasBase* to
CrasUtil, and check IsShuttingDown() in CrasUtil::CrasConnect(),
CrasUnifiedStream::Open(), and CrasInputStream::Open() to abort new CRAS
connections during shutdown.

Bug: 544339177
Test: autoninja -C out/Default media_unittests && ./out/Default/media_unittests --gtest_filter="AudioManagerCrasShutdownTest.ShutdownSkipsCrasConnections"
Test: crbug.com/544339177#comment7

HttpCache: Reject 206 responses with Content-Length: 0

PartialData::ResponseHeadersOK() previously checked
content_length->is_positive() before comparing Content-Length against
the Content-Range span, allowing Content-Length: 0 to pass even though a
valid 206 Content-Range always covers at least one byte.

Drop the is_positive() check so that Content-Length: 0 is rejected the
same way as any other mismatched Content-Length while still allowing
responses that omit Content-Length.

login: Merge UserAddingScreenImpl into UserAddingScreen

UserAddingScreenImpl in user_adding_screen.cc is the sole implementation
of UserAddingScreen in the codebase (originally separated to hide
base::Singleton<UserAddingScreenImpl>, which was removed in
https://crrev.com/c/8803594).

Collapse UserAddingScreenImpl and UserAddingScreen::Create() directly
into concrete ash::UserAddingScreen, and update
ChromeBrowserMainPartsAsh, ScreenLockerUnitTest, and
DemoLoginControllerTest to construct it via
std::make_unique<UserAddingScreen>(). This is a behavior-neutral cleanup
preparing to inject UserAddingScreen's dependencies.

TAG=agy

Ash: Migrate AmbientPhotoCacheTest off RunUntilIdle

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to modernize Ash tests by
migrating them away from RunUntilIdle() and toward targeted
synchronization with APIs such as RunUntil(), TestFuture, RunLoop
callbacks, observer waiters, or exact mock-time advancement.

Both RunUntilIdle() calls are deleted, along with the fixture's
wrapper. DownloadPhoto() and DownloadPhotoToTempFile() call
AmbientAccessTokenController::RequestAccessToken(), which synchronously
calls TestAmbientClient::RequestAccessToken() to store the pending
callback that IsAccessTokenRequestPending() checks. Nothing on that
path posts a task, so the check holds as soon as the call returns.

Skip view transition when new element becomes skipped content

If a captured new element becomes skipped content during the animation,
e.g. by content-visibility: hidden on an ancestor, skip the transition
instead of computing its geometry. This fixes a DCHECK failure in
PaintLayer.

Use SupervisedUserTestEnvironment in SupervisedUserBrowserUtilsTest

Replaces the calls to TestingProfile::SetIsSupervisedProfile and
TestingProfile::Builder::SetIsSupervisedProfile with
SupervisedUserTestEnvironment::EnableSupervisedAccount and
EnableRegularAccount, which toggle the supervision of the signed-in
account via the profile's IdentityManager instead.

Supervision is a property of the account rather than the profile:
SetIsSupervisedProfile only sets kSupervisedUserId, a pref that mirrors
the account's supervision status (see ChildAccountService).

ASH Refactoring: Add missing is_chromeos guards to CrOS-only strings

Ten strings in chrome/app/*.grd(p) were previously defined without an
is_chromeos guard (including two with unused macOS use_titlecase
duplicates) even though every reference in C++ was compiled only under
BUILDFLAG(IS_CHROMEOS).

Wrap those definitions in <if expr="is_chromeos"> and drop the two
unused use_titlecase duplicates.

TAG=agy

MiraclePtr: Mark wrapped_ptr [[maybe_unused]] in RawPtrNoOpImpl

Follow-up to crrev.com/c/8172284. Downstream consumers building
PartitionAlloc headers with -Werror -Wunused-parameter (such as
ChromeOS libchrome) fail on the named unused parameter in the no-op
ReportIfDangling() stub.

Mark wrapped_ptr with [[maybe_unused]], matching ReleaseWrappedPtr()
and Trace() in the same file.

23ced28618ba8b95d4cbc701d1209a741b476fbc [ diff ] 
Reland "[DBSC] Reject cross-site refresh session configs"

This is a reland of commit 9518f2b1c84612fd49990df31d61c4e5d8fe7558

Reason for reland: fixed the unit test failure caused by a parallel
clean-up CL https://crrev.com/c/8824036

Original change's description:
> [DBSC] Reject cross-site refresh session configs
>
> Previously, if a refresh request redirected to another site that
> returned a valid session configuration for itself, Chrome created a new
> session under that site sharing the original session's binding key while
> leaving the original session intact.
>
> Reject refresh responses that return a session configuration from a site
> different from the refresh endpoint's initial site with a fatal
> kCrossSiteRefreshSessionConfig error, deleting the existing session
> instead. Cross-site redirects that return an empty body, terminate the
> session with "continue": false, or redirect back to the original site
> remain allowed, as do same-site cross-origin refresh redirects and
> cross-site registrations.
>
> Guard the new restriction behind the enabled-by-default
> kDeviceBoundSessionsRequireSameSiteRefresh kill switch. A spec update
> will follow.
>
> Bug: 516490575
> Change-Id: Ia519d0d6cd806b3cfdf148c66c9c5e0a6a6a6964
> Reviewed-on: https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8821974
> Reviewed-by: Elly <ellyjones@chromium.org>
> Reviewed-by: Oussama Jaballah <oussamajb@google.com>
> Commit-Queue: Alex Ilin <alexilin@chromium.org>
> Reviewed-by: Alex Rudenko <alexrudenko@chromium.org>
> Cr-Commit-Position: refs/heads/main@{#1748174}

Bug: 516490575

5ac6c746204c996e0401267b2026ada55f6ca34b

5891050bd1c6a57423598d032e493f64970aee54
parent
97039f8e8d4d1011c7ff85977efba1c9324977e3 [ diff ] 
Report Storage Access API outcomes to Private Insights

RecordOutcomeSample() now also reports each Storage Access API request
outcome to the Private Insights Storage Access population, alongside the
existing DWA metric.

Reporting is a no-op unless kPrivateInsightsStorageAccess is enabled and
the profile has a PrivateInsightsService (none in Incognito, Guest or
system profiles); the service also requires UMA and UKM consent (parent
CL). The requesting site is reduced to its eTLD+1 using the ICANN
section of the Public Suffix List only, so e.g. alice.github.io is
reported as github.io. Sites without a known public registry (IP
addresses, localhost, single-label or unknown-TLD hosts, a host that is
itself a public suffix, opaque or non-HTTP sites) are not reported.
RequestOutcome is mapped to the proto enum explicitly, because the proto
reserves 0 for UNSPECIFIED, which the server pipeline drops. Only the
channel is set in the system profile. Not built in fuzzing builds.

📝 Commit Message (First Line)
Make the EventsDispatching perf test less GC-sensitive
📋 Metadata
commit
97039f8e8d4d1011c7ff85977efba1c9324977e3
[ log ]
author
Mason Freed <masonf@chromium.org>
Thu Nov 19 06:56:01 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Thu Nov 19 06:56:01 2026
tree
96d4aa70fc1ddc99dd4ee3346d8cbd41be398687
parent
7923202d65b70aed36801d407100c086ca279871 [ diff ] 
Make the EventsDispatching perf test less GC-sensitive

Every dispatchEvent() allocates its event path on the Oilpan heap, and
EventsDispatching dispatches ~1M events per second. With
measureRunsPerSecond(), each timed chunk ran for ~100ms, so it
contained a variable number of GCs (and incremental marking). The score
therefore depended on how GC scheduling lined up with the timed chunks,
which made it bimodal and sensitive to unrelated changes in the
pre-existing heap state, such as the one blamed in the bug.

This changes the test to time short batches of 20 runs (10,000
dispatches) and to force a GC, outside the timed region, before each
batch. A batch allocates well below the GC trigger threshold, so no GC
runs while dispatching. The result is still reported in runs/s over
750ms of timed dispatching.

Locally (content_shell, Linux), the old test dropped by ~18% partway
through each run, while per-batch times with this change stay tight
(p50 44ms, max 48ms); without the forced GCs, the same batches show GC
outliers up to ~85ms.

2d7cce6078950fd6897f5d4636d14ca808fa1f8a [ diff ] 
Fix blink::FixedPoint gdb pretty-printer for GDB 18.1.

In GDB 18.1, evaluating self.val['kFixedPointDenominator'] throws
"gdb.error: value has been optimized out" because FixedPoint has an
unsigned non-type template parameter and Chromium compiles with
-gsimple-template-names. GDB allegedly reconstructs the class name as
blink::FixedPoint<6, int> (without 'u'), which misses the DWARF cooked
index when value_static_field looks up the static constexpr member.

Compute the denominator directly from template_argument(0) instead.

Shrink LayoutTextFragment by 8 bytes on 64-bit

Move the bool to the end so that it no longer leaves padding before
content_string_. LayoutTextFragment goes from 136 to 128 bytes on
64-bit Windows and from 128 to 120 bytes on other 64-bit platforms;
32-bit is unchanged. It is used for generated content, list markers
and ::first-letter, e.g. 1.8K instances on an English Wikipedia
article.

baafa5cfeaaa3e9628e6ae036ee66ae8dc252657
parent
b3e45c8c2b7ff264579bb1d9babb86fc31121113 [ diff ] 
[MemoryCoordinator] Add traits for the upcoming BackgroundPolicy

Prepares for an upcoming BackgroundPolicy, which reduces the memory
usage of consumers while their process is backgrounded. Consumers will
opt in through two new traits:
- AffectedByBackgrounding: whether the consumer is affected when its
  process is backgrounded, and after which delay it is asked to release
  memory (~10 s, ~1 min, ~5 min). Nothing acts on it until the
  BackgroundPolicy lands.
- AffectedByMemoryPressure: lets a consumer opt out of
  MemoryPressureListenerPolicy, so that consumers which today only
  release memory when backgrounded can move to the BackgroundPolicy
  without also starting to respond to memory pressure.

A consumer with both traits set to kNo would never be affected by
either, so that combination is rejected: CHECKed in C++, asserted in
Java and refused during Mojo deserialization.

🌺 Disable consistently failing ContentTextSelectionTest on automotive

ContentTextSelectionTest#testCorrectSelectionMenuItemsAddedForInputSelection_dropdown
failed all retry attempts (hard-failed) in 4/20 recent builds on
chrome/ci/android-automotive-12l-x64-rel-tests (#24909..#24912).

Disabled here:
  ContentTextSelectionTest.testCorrectSelectionMenuItemsAddedForInputSelection_dropdown (automotive only)

Example failing run:
https://ci.chromium.org/b/8664751867294123073

Why disable rather than revert:
The regression was introduced by crrev.com/c/8722111, and skym's revert
crrev.com/c/8834365 is currently stalled on No-Unresolved-Comments and
Code-Owners. The author of 8722111 (alexmitra@) agreed to a temporary
disable on automotive while preparing a fix during London hours.

Hint for the follow-up fix:
Unlike testCorrectSelectionMenuItemsAddedForPlainTextSelection_dropdown
(which checks `if (shareAllowed)` and `if (webSearchAllowed)` before
expecting R.id.select_action_menu_share),
testCorrectSelectionMenuItemsAddedForInputSelection_dropdown
unconditionally expects R.id.select_action_menu_share, which is not
allowed on Automotive (expected:<10> but was:<9>).

To run/deflake this test on CQ before re-enabling, add the following
footer(s) to your CL description:
  Include-Ci-Only-Tests: internal.client.clank:android-automotive-12l-x64-rel-tests|content_shell_test_apk

This is mitigation, not a fix. The bug tracks the real investigation
and is assigned to alexmitra@.

parent
7661433e149009ac33ffb1debef240be8bd9c923 [ diff ] 
[CacheStorage] Truncate pre-existing entry streams on empty puts

This CL fixes CacheStorageCache and CacheStorageBlobToDiskCache so that
overwriting a reopened disk cache entry with an absent or zero-byte body
or side data always truncates pre-existing stream bytes.

When OpenOrCreateEntry reopens an unindexed disk cache entry (for
example, after an index mismatch), CacheStorageCache previously skipped
truncation when a stream had non-zero data because its size check was
inverted, and CacheStorageBlobToDiskCache completed zero-byte blobs
without issuing a truncating write. Together with rewriting headers to a
non-opaque response, this left a prior opaque response body readable.

Bug: 583238770

Revert "mb: Output gn_labels from analyze"

This reverts commit e5c1814b5054f62b522ff5192624e2f514db64d3.

Reason for revert: Nothing reads gn_labels. filter.analyze_targets() in the recipes was its only user, and it is reverted. gn check now maps the compile targets to GN labels with mb check.

Original change's description:
> mb: Output gn_labels from analyze
>
> "mb analyze" returns compile_targets as ninja targets (labels without
> "//") and test_targets as the input names. Add gn_labels, the GN labels
> of both, so the recipe can run "gn check --check-generated" on what the
> build compiled without mapping names back to labels. Like
> compile_targets, gn_labels is ["all"] when it would contain "all".
>
> compile_targets is now computed in local variables and assigned once.
>
> Bug: 584979771

[a11y] Create the mock WCAX first in WebContentsAccessibilityE2ETest

WebContentsAccessibilityE2ETest#setupTest() intermittently failed with
"Failed to find expected node after HTML setup", mostly on Android
Automotive builders. This fixes two problems in the test setup.

1. AccessibilityTestService#waitForCondition() ran its immediate check
   (for waitForNode(), a full tree walk over IPC) before registering the
   event listener. Events that arrived during that walk were only
   cached, and waitForNode() does not consult the event cache, so it
   then waited for an event that never came. Register the listener
   before the immediate check.

2. The accessibility service is enabled before Content Shell launches,
   so Android creates a real WebContentsAccessibilityImpl as soon as the
   window appears, and that instance turns on accessibility for the
   page. setupTest() then replaced it with a Mockito mock, which left
   two instances: the real one was removed from the WebContents' user
   data but not destroyed, so it could still receive the page's
   accessibility tree and events, and building the mock blocked the UI
   thread while the tree was being built. Add setFactoryForTesting() to
   WebContentsAccessibilityImpl and have the test install a factory that
   builds the mock before launching Content Shell, so the first and only
   instance is the mock. No blank page or swap is needed.

With setup fixed, re-enable the tests that were restricted or disabled
on Android Automotive because of these setup failures, and
testAccessibilityServiceReceivesInitialEvent_SdkBalklavaAndAbove, which
was disabled for the same setup failure (crbug.com/529689125).

Earlier patchsets used a blank-page setup and swapped in the mock
afterwards; this follows the review suggestion to create the mock first.

[Immersive Mode] Add entry method and per-session UMA metrics

Adds UMA histograms to measure how users enter and interact with Browser
immersive mode sessions:

- Entry Method Split (Android.ImmersiveMode.EntryMethod): Records
  whether Browser immersive mode was entered via the 3-dot app menu
  fullscreen button or keyboard shortcut (note: F11 shortcut wiring will
  be added in a follow-up CL).
- Navigations per Session (Android.ImmersiveMode.NavigationsPerSession):
  Records the count of committed cross-document navigations within a
  session, excluding reloads and New Tab Page loads.
- Tab Switches per Session
  (Android.ImmersiveMode.TabSwitchesPerSession): Records the count of
  tab switches while remaining in a session.
- Session Duration (Android.ImmersiveMode.SessionDuration): Records the
  total duration of each session when exiting Browser immersive mode.

345290fb1dba93d98ca301d9f2ba622d0e13c289 [ diff ] 
[omnibox][webui] Make fast tabs wait for the latest results

Pressing enter before the results for the latest input arrive waits for
them before navigating. E.g. quickly typing 'goo<enter>' navigates to
'goo's default match.

But tab didn't wait. E.g. with a 'ccs' site search shortcut, quickly
typing 'ccs<tab>' focused the AI Mode button, because the results for
'cc' had no keyword chip.

With this CL, tab and shift+tab wait for the latest results too, so
'ccs<tab>' enters keyword mode.

WebUI Toolbar: Add PrioritizeWebUINavigationURLLoader to enabled.sh

This CL adds PrioritizeWebUINavigationURLLoader to enabled.sh and
syncs chrome/browser/about_flags.cc via generate_chrome_flags.py. A
follow-up CL syncs tools/perf/ for separate OWNERS approval.

PrioritizeWebUINavigationURLLoader is already enabled in
WebUIReloadButtonStudy in fieldtrial_testing_config.json, where it
binds navigation WebUIURLLoaderFactory receivers onto the high-priority
navigation network response task runner. Passing explicit
--enable-features flags without it caused Variations to skip
WebUIReloadButtonStudy, leaving initial NTP WebUI loads on the default
UI task queue while browser compositor tasks were prioritized on
startup.

[PA] Auto remove extra whitespace from embedded JSON

Adding PA_JSON_STRING macro lets the compiler remove
extra whitespace at runtime. So for example

```
PA_JSON_STRING({
  "abc": {
     "def": 123
  }
})
```

gets embedded as `{ "abc": { "def": 123 } }` saving a
few bytes.

e25899b7e9f5c40f5eb1d08cb88b42b1ac718694 [ diff ] 
Translator API: get the TranslateKit library from a LibrarySource

Move library lookup behind TranslateKitLibrarySource, with
ComponentUpdaterLibrarySource for component updater and
FixedPathLibrarySource for --translate-kit-binary-path.

RegisterTranslateKitComponent now clears prefs::kTranslateKitBinaryPath
with ClearPref() on registration to restore the default state instead of
persisting an empty user value, and SetBinaryPathInPrefs() checks that
install_dir is absolute. Init() also accepts a null callback to skip
duplicate callbacks on repeat create().

Bug: 428227642

Migrate android-mte-arm64-rel from Pixel 8 to Pixel 9

Switch android-mte-arm64-rel and its mirrored trybuilder from the
Pixel 8 (shiba) pool to the 14 dedicated Pixel 9 (tokay) devices on
Android 15 (AP4A.241205.013) provisioned in b/362879099.

Exclude the small set of tests that fail deterministically on Pixel 9 /
Android 15: system TTC font lookup, scroll-offset and persistent-cache
content_browsertests, sequential threadsafe death tests that exceed the
per-test timeout while dumping MTE tags, and ANGLE's
PageFaultHandlerProtect (fixed upstream in crrev.com/c/8829389).

a609940d502ae88e65d707642c9ca5b2d148fa38 [ diff ] 
ASH Refactoring: Copy forwarding chromeos_strings.h to gen dir

After crrev.com/c/8817655 moved grit to //chromeos/resources,
//chromeos/strings:strings_grit exposed a checked-in header via
source_set. On incremental bots, a stale generated
gen/chromeos/strings/grit/chromeos_strings.h remained on disk and
shadowed it because -Igen precedes -I../.., leaving callers with
outdated string IDs.

Use copy("strings_grit") to copy the forwarding header into
$target_gen_dir/grit/chromeos_strings.h so incremental builds overwrite
the stale header and recompile dependents.

TAG=agy
CONV=ddf79353-0582-4c1a-b8b3-b24d9c7503be

0920b2e7018c01888329f6e628805ec36324478e [ diff ] 
Convert CHECK back to DCHECK in PenEventProcessor

When pen events with mismatching eraser pointer IDs are processed, the
CHECK in PenEventProcessor::GenerateEvent triggers and causes a crash.

This change converts the CHECK back to a DCHECK to avoid crashes in
production when processing eraser pen events with mismatching pointer
IDs.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/8c356d7208400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

Revert IsLeaf CHECK back to DCHECK in RangeFromPoint

In ui::AXPlatformNodeTextProviderWin::RangeFromPoint, nearest_node might
not be a leaf node in certain release build scenarios, causing a fatal
CHECK crash. Revert the CHECK back to a DCHECK until we are confident it
will not trigger in production.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/c6cb40b208400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

[🌱][Android] Enable gardening on android-17-x64-rel

https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/android-17-x64-rel

android-17-x64-rel has been green on every build since the move to
chromium.android (18 in a row, after a tree-wide compile break), with
DCHECKs on and shard retries absorbing the occasional flake. Put it on
the Android gardener rotation, make it tree-closing and enable it on
branches, matching android-16-x64-rel. Also add it to the Test Reviver
Android emulator launcher, so tests disabled on it get re-run.

tree
8415a749f7d4257c830dbcc64c613e5477499d2b
parent
b93326b7ac95c614df276201a52919a2f97b8a3b [ diff ] 
Reland "Remove WidgetObserver::OnWidgetClosing from WebUIBubbleDialogView"

This relands commit d18477df028088b099717aa29e7e37a3be00e3e3.

The original change crashed in tests where the profile was destroyed
while the WebUI bubble widget was closing asynchronously:
WebUIContentsWrapper::OnProfileWillBeDestroyed() reset web_contents_
before the window closing callback ran ClearContentsWrapper(), causing a
null pointer dereference on
contents_wrapper_->web_contents()->WasHidden().

This reland guards contents_wrapper_->web_contents() before calling
WasHidden(), ensures WebUIBubbleManager::CloseBubble() clears the
contents wrapper synchronously. In addition, this change update the
Widget to be client own.

bde69fc4840942a797eba53b93a0f398536af4ce
parent
b400392e665f3c8fd24d15e62d1c594c9d5cd24e [ diff ] 
printing: Stop routing Android printing through PrinterQuery

Android previously routed window.print() through PrinterQuery solely to
open the Java print dialog and then immediately discarded the query.
This split the scripted print lifecycle across PrinterQuery,
PrintingContextAndroid, and PrintViewManagerBasic while pulling
Android-specific JNI and TabAndroid dependencies into printer_query.cc.

Drive the system print dialog directly from
PrintViewManagerBasic::SetupScriptedPrintAndroid() via an atomic start
contract that validates Activity and controller state upfront. Bind the
pending IPC reply to the requesting frame's lifecycle until dialog
teardown. Since that reply now waits for teardown, dispatch
finishPrint() from the Java session even when initiatePrint() never ran.

[Reading mode PDFs] Rename GetImageForOcr() to GetImageBitmap()

Prep work for the raster image feature in Reading Mode. This function
will no longer be exclusively used for OCR and will now also be used to
fetch the bitmap given a page index and page object by
PdfAccessibilityTree, so rename it to a name that fits and expose it
unconditionally outside ENABLE_SCREEN_AI_SERVICE.

089135530b1a430fff2f6e754129eadadf66092d
parent
bff07b76f229dab16c6201bd1fbfe5026fcbe0cb [ diff ] 
🛡 [Site Search] Keep TemplateUrl out of settings menus and dialogs

A Java TemplateUrl wraps a native TemplateURL that TemplateUrlService
may delete at any time. The Site search settings mediators captured
the TemplateUrl of a row in its overflow menu delegate and click
handlers, and SiteSearchDialogCoordinator captured it in the save and
remove-confirmation callbacks, on the assumption that the list refresh
in onTemplateURLServiceChanged() replaces everything that refers to
it. It does not: a row view that is on its way out, an open menu and
an open dialog all keep their callbacks and can run them after the
native object is gone.

Read what the menus need (keyword, starter pack state, providing
extension id) when the list item is created, while the TemplateUrl is
known to be valid, and let the deferred code work with those values
only. Edit and remove requests now carry the keyword, and
SiteSearchDialogCoordinator looks the search engine up again when it
handles them, doing nothing if it no longer exists. The dialogs keep
the keyword instead of the TemplateUrl. All actions were already
addressed to TemplateUrlService by keyword, so their targets are
unchanged. This follows the approach taken for SearchEngineAdapter.

[Public Transit] Add CtaPageStation#closeLastTabToTabSwitcherFast()

Add CtaPageStation#closeLastTabToTabSwitcherFast(), the programmatic
version of
TabSwitcherActionMenuFacility#selectCloseTabAndDisplayTabSwitcher(). It
closes the last tab with TabClosingSource.GRID_TAB_SWITCHER, like the
"Close tab" menu item, so the window stays open. Both reach the same
Outcome, RegularTabSwitcherStation#afterClosingLastTab(): the empty tab
switcher.

Use it in 5 tests instead of ChromeTabUtils#closeCurrentTab(), which
uses TabClosingSource.UNKNOWN and so closes the window on S+.

AskBeforeHttpDialogTest#testFailedUpgrade_WarningShown_CloseTab stays
on ChromeTabUtils for now: on desktop, the toolbar can stay displayed
over the empty tab switcher after the tab closes with the dialog
showing, so the Trip times out.

parent
5443dacfdf6ac291673a13d4baee49d1871ce5b3 [ diff ] 
[Gardener] Disable CommandsApiTest.IncognitoShortcutsIgnoredWhenNotAllowed on Linux Wayland

CommandsApiTest.IncognitoShortcutsIgnoredWhenNotAllowed (added in
https://crrev.com/c/8792186) deterministically times out on
linux-wayland-mutter-rel-tests, linux-wayland-weston-rel-tests, and
linux-arm64-wayland-rel-fyi.

After opening the incognito window, the test calls
ui_test_utils::BringBrowserWindowToFront() to re-activate the regular
window, which hangs until timeout because Wayland compositors refuse
programmatic window activation without an activation token from user
input.

Filter the test out on Mutter and Weston while keeping the fix for
crbug.com/568850674 and crbug.com/563525847 landed.

Bug: 587583567

🌺 Revert "Keep SpaceSplitString alive during ElementRuleCollector class loop"

This reverts commit 0b6bd58886ec7da4dd6da2dff845cc70c2acc5c8.
Culprit: 0b6bd58886ec - "Keep SpaceSplitString alive during ElementRuleCollector class loop" (refs/heads/main@{#1748810}, https://crrev.com/c/8816006)

Reason for revert: consistently fails chromium/ci/android-10-x86-rel
(and 8 other Android builders).

Mechanism: CL 8816006 introduced
ElementRuleCollectorTest.MutateClassDuringRuleMatching, which calls
SetBodyInnerHTML(...) (running UpdateAllLifecyclePhasesForTest() and
clearing validity_is_dirty_ = false) before installing the
RegExp.prototype.exec hook. On dcheck_always_on = true builds (CQ
trybots), DCHECK_EQ(is_valid_, (!WillValidate() || Valid())) at
listed_element.cc:707 re-evaluates Valid() as a side effect and fires
the hook, masking the bug. On non-DCHECK Release CI builds across
Linux/Mac/ChromeOS/Android (9 CI bots, 12/12 runs), DCHECK_EQ compiles
out and IsValidElement() returns the cached is_valid_ directly without
invoking RegExp.prototype.exec, causing EXPECT_TRUE(g_callback_fired) to
fail deterministically. Dirtying validity after installing the hook
(e.g., input->SetNeedsValidityCheck()) will allow relanding cleanly.

ElementRuleCollectorTest.MutateClassDuringRuleMatching has failed in 1/1
recent builds of chromium/ci/android-10-x86-rel (and 12 failing verdicts
across 9 builders overall), every attempt in every failing run. This is
not flake: it reproduces consistently, blocking the CQ for unrelated CLs
that touch this suite. The regression appears at
refs/heads/main@{#1748810}.

Example failing run:
https://ci.chromium.org/b/8664742678838198753

To run/deflake this test on CQ before relanding, add the following
footers to your CL description:
  Cq-Include-Trybots: luci.chromium.try:android-10-x86-rel
  Include-Ci-Only-Tests: chromium.android:android-10-x86-rel|blink_unittests

Reverting to green the tree first. Please re-land once the failure is
understood and covered; this revert is about restoring CQ throughput,
not about the merit of the change.

Original change's description:
> Keep SpaceSplitString alive during ElementRuleCollector class loop
>
> ElementRuleCollector::CollectMatchingRulesInternal iterates
> element.ClassNames() with a range-for loop. During nested rule matching
> (such as matching an input element against :invalid, which validates
> its pattern attribute via ScriptRegexp), a re-entrant callback can
> mutate the element's class attribute and trigger garbage collection. If
> element.ClassNames() is not held by value on the stack, the element's
> old SpaceSplitString::Data loses its strong reference and gets
> finalized by cppgc, causing a heap use-after-free when the class loop
> advances to the next class name.
>
> This patch takes a copy-by-value of element.ClassNames() before the
> loop so its Member<SpaceSplitString::Data> remains rooted on the stack
> throughout iteration, preventing the data from being collected
> prematurely.
>
> Fixed: 584336207

[remoting host] Respect OS text scale in PrivacyOverlayMac

Scales PrivacyOverlayMac backdrop and Disconnect button fonts at session
start with the user's macOS Accessibility text size preference:

- Queries the com.apple.universalaccess FontSizeCategory preference
  (per-app bundle override or global tier, using empirically measured
  macOS 14-15 Body point sizes clamped to [1.0, 2.5]) and caches the
  resolved scale on PrivacyOverlayMac during BeginOverlay().
- Scales backdrop title and sharing message typography, shifting stacked
  elements downward on short displays so the icon, title, and message
  never overlap at up to 2.5x scale, and replaces
  sharingTextRectForBounds: with contentLayoutForBounds:.
- Updates PrivacyOverlayButtonWindow to scale button typography, cap
  window width to the target display bounds while preserving the full
  VoiceOver accessibilityLabel, expand best-effort capture-exclusion
  bounds before growing, and apply size and centered origin in a single
  setFrame: call.
- Adds hermetic unit tests injecting an NSUserDefaults suite to exercise
  FontSizeCategory parsing (global, per-app override, and UseGlobal) and
  short/narrow display layout clamping at 2.5x scale.

📝 Commit Message (First Line)
Don't scroll when a focused input's list target changes
📋 Metadata
commit
8ba74f12d641e46173c7bbb126ff6128f0883f1c
[ log ]
author
Mason Freed <masonf@chromium.org>
Thu Nov 19 03:17:47 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Thu Nov 19 03:17:47 2026
tree
e90529d7198df9c85f35b6dd26ecaa725e2d8ca9
parent
4b1b4baf0b69a0a0543235855c7579d70f7d94df [ diff ] 
Don't scroll when a focused input's list target changes

TextFieldInputType::ListAttributeTargetChanged() can be called
synchronously from DOM mutation notifications (Element::UpdateId()
during InsertedInto()/RemovedFrom(), or
HTMLDataListElement::ChildrenChanged()). When it restructured the inner
editor of a focused input, it called UpdateSelectionOnFocus(kRestore),
which scrolled the selection into view and therefore forced a
synchronous style and layout update in the middle of the mutation.
During moveBefore(), the moved subtree is still attached but out of the
tree, and its dirty slot assignment has been removed from the recalc
queue, so this nested layout could recalc slot assignments in place
while LocalFrameView was iterating scroll_anchoring_scrollable_areas_,
leading to a use-after-free.

Restore the cached selection without scrolling (preventScroll), which
avoids the forced update. The selection itself is still restored.

Note, this CL is still needed, even if/when the CL stack at
https://crrev.com/c/8733581 lands. Once that stack lands, this bug
would be seen as a crash with `kNoRecalcWhileForbidden`.

tree
00545915fc37c0b2ccb294c1f8b6664f479ecb06
parent
74b81e39cda27efc320801c671171f16a49e2cff [ diff ] 
[TopChrome] Decouple IsTopChromeWebUIURL() from .top-chrome URL suffix

IsTopChromeWebUIURL() decides whether a WebUI is set up for the shared
Top Chrome WebUI renderer process (e.g. the --top-chrome-webui renderer
flag, bypassing spare renderers and WebUI bundled code caching). It
currently checks that the URL is chrome://*.top-chrome.

Since https://crrev.com/c/8689185, whether a WebUI shares that process
is determined by WebUIConfig::process_lock_host() rather than by the
URL suffix, and since https://crrev.com/c/8739542 all callers pass the
WebUI's site URL rather than the shared process lock URL
(chrome://top-chrome/).

This CL changes IsTopChromeWebUIURL() to check that the WebUIConfig
registered for the URL specifies "top-chrome" as its process lock host,
using WebUIConfigMap::GetProcessLockHost()
(https://crrev.com/c/8752739). Because WebUIConfigMap is only available
in the browser process, this CL also moves webui_url_utils.{h,cc} from
//chrome/common to //chrome/browser/ui/webui. All of its users are in
//chrome/browser.

Every WebUIConfig that specifies "top-chrome" as its process lock host
is hosted at chrome://*.top-chrome and vice versa, so this CL does not
change the behavior for registered WebUIs. Unregistered
chrome://*.top-chrome URLs are no longer treated as Top Chrome WebUI
URLs.

This partially unblocks removing the .top-chrome suffix from Top Chrome
WebUI URLs (crbug.com/434151253).

76049bb3c233844f4e2a0ad43ecc52a73ca9f7af [ diff ] 
settings: Tie the important sites dialog to Delete browsing data

Context:
Delete browsing data (ClearBrowsingDataFragment) asks about important
sites in ConfirmImportantSitesDialogFragment before deleting cookies or
cache. The dialog was shown in the page's parent FragmentManager and
reported back through setTargetFragment() and onActivityResult().

Problem:
Finishing the page pops it off its back stack (SettingsActivity) or
replaces it (settings in a tab), which removes only the page from its
container and leaves the dialog up, still targeting the destroyed page.
Clicking "Delete data" twice before the dialog appears queues two
dialogs, so confirming one leaves the other behind. Confirming that one
used to silently delete the data again from the dead page, and since
crrev.com/c/8564699 it crashes in requireActivity().

Solution:
Show the dialog as a child of the page, so that it goes away with it,
and only once. Report the user's choice through the Fragment Result API,
which only delivers it while the page is started, instead of the
deprecated target fragment.

d40ab91e7707d79a88e20e101a88f667b093442e

31f35ca29264dfdbe3fbbe476a915acd3da27adc [ diff ] 
weston, mutter: Build as C11

Both weston and mutter fail to build as C23:

weston (upstream builds as gnu99):
- shared/helpers.h defines an unreachable() macro, which collides with
  the unreachable() macro that C23's <stddef.h> defines
  (-Wmacro-redefined).
- libweston.h puts WL_DEPRECATED after function declarators. In C23,
  wayland-util.h defines WL_DEPRECATED as [[deprecated]], which isn't
  allowed in that position ("'deprecated' attribute cannot be applied
  to types").

mutter:
- libmutter relies on -Wno-implicit-function-declaration for gbm
  functions missing from the sysroot (crbug.com/396647882). C23 removes
  implicit function declarations completely, making those calls hard
  errors.

Add -std=c11 to weston_config and libmutter_config so both keep building
as C11 when Chromium's default C standard moves to C23. Both configs are
appended after the default compiler config, so their -std flag takes
precedence.

388749936f194da71a04082ff66d50ae0b2147c2 [ diff ] 
[ash] skyvault: take user_manager::User* in MigrationNotificationManager

MigrationNotificationManager's constructor narrows from
content::BrowserContext* to user_manager::User*, CHECK_DEREFed into the
`user_` member. This class's own domain is already User-identity-shaped:
CreateNotificationPtr()/CloseNotification() (both free functions it
already called) take a const user_manager::User& and use
user.GetAccountId()/user.username_hash() to build notification ids --
`user_` is used 6 times, versus the 2 remaining Profile needs
(ShowItemInFolder(), ShowSignInNotification()), both served on demand
via a new profile() helper.

MigrationNotificationManagerFactory now declares ProfileSelection::kNone
for Ash Internals (sign-in/lock-screen) profiles -- the one profile type
with no associated user, and SkyVault migration never runs for it anyway
-- rather than building the service and discovering that at runtime.
With Regular the only profile type left, which always has a user,
BuildServiceInstanceForBrowserContext() CHECKs it instead of passing the
context straight through.

MockMigrationNotificationManager (test double) updated to match,
forwarding its own pointer-typed parameter straight through to the base
class. The 3 call sites across local_files_migration_manager_
unittest.cc and _browsertest.cc already had a user() helper on hand, so
they switch from passing profile() to user() directly.

2dd9040f2a444135aeb855e2c57a0eaf862e1c1f [ diff ] 
[iOS] Re-enable cross-device sign-in deep link tests

Re-enable `testCrossDeviceSigninSignedInTargetAccountNotOnDevice` and
`testCrossDeviceSigninSignedInTargetAccountOnDevice` in
`DeeplinkSigninTestCase`.

Both tests were disabled in `deeplink_signin_egtest.mm` in
https://crrev.com/c/8177903 (https://crbug.com/540818565) and suppressed
in `ios/testing/test_expectations.txt` in https://crrev.com/c/8178083
(https://crbug.com/540811503) and https://crrev.com/c/8178425
(https://crbug.com/540829196) because opening a cross-device sign-in
deep link right after signing in an account only deferred
`-showSignin:baseViewController:` via `dispatch_async()` on the main
queue. If an in-flight modal dismissal from the preceding sign-in was
still running, the new sign-in or account-switch presentation was
silently dropped.

In https://crrev.com/c/8355245, `CrossDeviceSigninSceneAgent` (with
`switches::kCrossDeviceSigninDismissModals`, enabled in
`DeeplinkSigninTestCase`) was updated to call `-[SceneCommands
dismissModalDialogsWithCompletion:]` before presenting the sign-in
coordinator, ensuring any in-flight modal dismissals finish first.

https://crrev.com/c/8691915 removed the `DISABLED_` prefixes in
`deeplink_signin_egtest.mm` while leaving the gardening entries in
`ios/testing/test_expectations.txt`. Remove those remaining `[ Pass
Failure ]` entries in `ios/testing/test_expectations.txt` to fully
re-enable both tests.

The tests were verified on physical `iPhone 13` and physical `iPad
(A16)`.

Part of http://go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome for AI week.

Fixed: 540811503

[Focus] Apply Focus Theme When Ephemeral Tab Group Is Promoted

Promoting the focused ephemeral (non-group focus) tab group left the
browser unthemed and the Unfocus button in its ephemeral colors, because
the group's visuals are applied while it is still ephemeral and the tab
strip did not react to the promotion. Both tab strip implementations now
handle OnTabGroupPromoted() by applying the focus mode theme, which also
refreshes the ephemeral state cached by the group's views.

Add browser tests that promote a focused ephemeral group through the
promotion bubble and through TabStripModel, covering the browser theme
on the legacy tab strip, and the browser theme and Unfocus button color
on the unified tab strip in both orientations.

parent
cbeddb2ad2d19336c850be58710dd25a3c63948f [ diff ] 
webnn: add Cartesian rank-range validation for multi-input operators

The single-operand helpers only vary one operand's rank at a time. For
operators whose inputs carry independent rankRange entries, accepting
each rank in isolation does not imply accepting every combination, so
add two Cartesian in-range helpers to resources/utils_validation.js:

 - validateTwoOperandsRankInRange iterates every (rank1, rank2) pair for
   a two-input operator, picking a dtype both operands declare that is
   also creatable as a graph input.
 - validateNOperandsRankInRange generalises this to N operands, picking
   each operand's dtype independently via pickSupportedDataTypeInfo so
   operands with narrower domains (e.g. where's uint8-only condition)
   get a type they actually accept.

Every dim is kExampleDimSize, so operands of any independent ranks
broadcast trivially.

Apply them to element-wise binary, element-wise logical, matmul and
where, each combined with validateOperandRankOutOfRange per operand so
the out-of-range regions stay covered without duplicating the Cartesian
in-range pass.

[omnibox] Show the AIM hint in the full WebUI omnibox popup

Like the Views omnibox, the popup's focused, empty input now shows the
AIM hint ("Press tab then enter to ask AI Mode") while the AIM button
is visible. The searchbox asks the browser for the hint at most once
per focus session via a new `PageHandler.RequestAimHint()`, which
applies the existing impression limits and records the impression.

With the full WebUI omnibox enabled (`IsFullWebUIOmnibox()`),
`OmniboxViewViews::OnPaint()` no longer records impressions, since the
popup records them. It is unchanged otherwise.

tree

parent
8adec11ef43520639f0e6afe074556467c5276d6 [ diff ] 
[Unbounded] Put render passes inside an unbounded element in its frame

LayerTreeHostImpl::CalculateRenderPasses() put only the unbounded
element's own pass into the unbounded CompositorFrame. A render surface
inside it, e.g. a pressed button's ripple, had its pass in the main
frame but its RenderPassDrawQuad in the unbounded pass, so Viz rejected
the unbounded frame. This isn't platform specific, but only shows on
Mac, where the unbounded window draws nothing else: the omnibox popup
went blank while a button in it was pressed.

Put the pass of each render surface whose render target chain reaches
an unbounded element's surface into the unbounded frame, if that
surface draws a pass itself (it is the frame's root).

a39e787365920bb672d5e3e553b3ea33328c383f [ diff ] 
[omnibox][webui] Fix Mac cursors over browser UI

Note: All of this is behind the new kMacCursorUpdatesUnderKeyChildWindow
killswitch. Windows that don't opt in behave as before. Cursors set by
web page content are not covered here.

With the Full WebUI omnibox focused on Mac, hovering browser Views kept
the arrow cursor. The vertical tab strip's resize handle, for example,
never showed the resize cursor.

Focusing the omnibox makes its popup, a child NSWindow, the key window.
AppKit only sends -cursorUpdate: to the key window, and
-[ViewsNSWindowDelegate setCursor:] only sets the cursor directly when
an ancestor window is key, so the browser window's cursor was stored but
never applied.

A NativeWidgetMacNSWindow can now return YES from the new
ancestorsUpdateCursorWhileKey, and OmniboxPopupNSWindow does. While such
a window is key:
- Its ancestors set their cursor directly when the cursor changes.
- An ancestor holding mouse capture sets its cursor again when the
  window becomes key. Pressing the resize handle hands key status to the
  browser window and back to the popup with the button down, and AppKit
  resets the cursor on that change.

HID: Migrate ChromeHidConnection to JniRawPtr
📋 Metadata

a2052effbbb3d91a5f4ae7a80e0a4216c62d6343

tree
c8a6a38a395cf128b9c01aee0c7c9f6a1a18484c
parent
1c697d8eb0bd460e4d0866170f3194eef46854e3 [ diff ] 
HID: Migrate ChromeHidConnection to JniRawPtr

ChromeHidConnection stores its native ::device::HidConnectionAndroid
pointer in a raw Java long field. Passing untyped integer handles across
the JNI boundary bypasses compile-time type checking and lifetime
tracking, risking use-after-free bugs when the native object is
destroyed.

Declare a package-private NativeHidConnectionAndroid JniTypeToken mapped
to ::device::HidConnectionAndroid and store the borrowed reference in a
JniRawPtr<NativeHidConnectionAndroid> created via jni_zero::MakeRaw.
Pass the reference to @NativeMethods calls via
JniPtr<NativeHidConnectionAndroid> and release the handle on native
teardown.

2dc8b5734667011b5e1b4222ee9336bbf179e7f5 [ diff ] 
updater: Use designated initializers for AppCompletionInfo

HandleInstallResult() builds the AppCompletionInfo of the installed app
by assigning its fields one at a time in an if/else chain. Build it with
designated initializers instead: MakeAppCompletionInfo() returns the
entry for each update state, and GetAppCompletionCode() maps the error
code of the install to a completion code. Behavior doesn't change.

Convert the HandleInstallResult() test cases to designated initializers,
name them, add cases for a launch command, an installer error and the
three reboot codes, and check every field of the app entry that
HandleInstallResult() sets. Use a designated initializer in
WebView2ProgressWndTest.CloseOnlyAfterCompletion.

parent
3f51ae19c2743f77c119f0c9c2017d2ddacb7139 [ diff ] 
[ash-ime] Harden language pack downloads in ImeServiceConnector

ImeServiceConnector previously downloaded language packs at net::IDLE
priority without retries, wrote directly to the final archive path
(exposing partial files and risking delayed-delete races when
superseded), and left in-flight Mojo callbacks unresolved when the
profile was destroyed.

Raise download priority to net::LOW, enable SimpleURLLoader retries on
transient 5xx/DNS/network-change errors, download to a per-attempt
temp file before atomically replacing on a USER_VISIBLE sequence, log
failures, and resolve callbacks on profile destruction.

Bug: b:587502948
Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter=ImeServiceConnectorTest.*

CONV=a82f3e52-8852-4c91-9a2b-4c82e9bafaf9

Reland "[webui] Enable bundled code cache generation on linux-chromeos"

This is a reland of commit 909f9af1322d4ff9fab8a0616d62abb80d92a2f9

The original change was reverted in https://crrev.com/c/8812193
because `code_cache_generator` hit an ASan odr-violation on
"Libfuzzer Upload Chrome OS ASan". https://crrev.com/c/8828436
fixes it. The diff is unchanged.

Original change's description:
> [webui] Enable bundled code cache generation on linux-chromeos
>
> Enable build-time WebUI bundled V8 code cache generation for ChromeOS
> builds where host_toolchain == current_toolchain, such as linux-chromeos
> and the linux-chromeos-rel CQ builder.
>
> Device cross-compiles (cros:target and cros:host) stay off because the
> generator links Blink. Cross-compilation would build the generator in
> cros:v8_snapshot, where nothing builds Blink today because ChromeOS
> disables use_v8_context_snapshot.
>
> Runtime use stays gated. The feature kWebUIBundledCodeCache is disabled
> by default (content/public/common/content_features.cc). Only
> WebUIReloadButtonStudy turns it on (fieldtrial_testing_config.json, with
> chromeos added in crrev.com/c/8739342), and toolbar WebUI loads only
> under that study. On linux-chromeos, this change updates resources.pak
> contents without altering runtime behavior until the study or feature
> is turned on.
>

9899829e3ceb923afbe1c4d52b0c708174b9c7e0 [ diff ] 
[iOS] Convert ScreenType from NS_ENUM to enum class

Migrate `ScreenType` to a C++ scoped `enum class` in accordance with the
Bling style guide, since all consumers are Objective-C++ translation
units and no Objective-C or Swift bridging is required:

- Convert `ScreenType` to `enum class ScreenType` and qualify or bring
  its enumerators into scope across First Run and Sign-in callers.
- Store `std::vector<ScreenType>` instead of `NSArray<NSNumber*>*` in
  `ScreenProvider` to avoid boxing and integer casts at screen sequence
  construction and iteration sites.

tree
22fa227621f18b006abfbbe3551e0add996b9d4a
parent
d4be14b0a32bbb1fd2f224f7dc4a403367a956bf [ diff ] 
[Wayland] Send text-input-v3 state without waiting for done

With fcitx5 on niri or sway, the input method indicator popup that is
shown when switching input methods appeared in the bottom-left corner
or at a stale caret position.

ZwpTextInputV3Impl only sent set_cursor_rectangle, set_surrounding_text
and set_content_type once a done event arrived whose serial matched the
number of commits. The protocol does not require compositors to answer
each commit with done, and wlroots (sway) and Smithay (niri) only send
done when the input method commits. After a text-input commit, any
later cursor rectangle, surrounding text or content type was therefore
held until the next input method commit. Caret moves and field switches
never reached the input method, which kept using the last committed
cursor rectangle, or none at all.

Send and commit state requests right away, like GTK does, and drop the
pending vs committed split and the commit and done serial counters. The
serial was only used to hold back requests. Incoming events were
already applied regardless of it.

This deviates from the client-side SHOULD in the description of the
done event in text-input-unstable-v3.xml, which says that pending
set_* requests should be sent and committed after receiving a done
event with a matching serial. Following it only works if the
compositor answers every text-input commit with done, which wlroots and
Smithay do not, so it leaves the input method with stale state there.
GTK's gtkimcontextwayland.c also commits state immediately and only
uses the serial to decide when to apply incoming events, so compositors
and input methods already handle this.

This also fixes the omnibox case where the surrounding text was queued
until the next done event and then dropped by Reset(), since nothing is
queued any more.

Fixed: 491869953, 565605611

254beb6397516e336949cfdd8da24d1d911252fb
parent
3494a5b702234c057ee8b036a9e69850af6b4638 [ diff ] 
[iOS] Handle synchronous completion in FirstRunCoordinator

Prevent crashing on nil view controller presentation and dropped
completion callbacks when all First Run screens skip presentation
synchronously during startup (for example, when the sign-in screen
auto-skips because the user is already signed in and no subsequent
screens remain):

- Guard post-screen presentation and follow-up work via a weak self
  reference so synchronous teardown and owner release inside
  didFinishFirstRun() do not access a stopped or deallocated
  coordinator, or attempt to present a nil navigation controller.
- Post the completion callback on the current task runner when
  stopWithCompletion() runs before the navigation controller is
  presented, preserving the asynchronous dismissal contract expected by
  FirstRunProfileAgent.
- Record FirstRun.Stage kStart before kComplete when First Run finishes
  synchronously prior to presenting the navigation controller.

cf9743ab0dfb7acb011de886c3ce54e7011271c9

tree
10932ad91ea54068c9376ce9453ea1ee9d7fa09f
parent
fa15d1b342021bd28babfcd0873f1bc3e8c3a227 [ diff ] 
[WebNN] Reject createExportableTensor early when interop is unsupported

`MLContext::createExportableTensor()` unconditionally called
`SharedImageInterface::CreateSharedImageForMLTensor()` with
`SHARED_IMAGE_USAGE_WEBNN_SHARED_TENSOR` before sending
`CreateTensorFromMailbox()` to the backend.

On platforms without a `SharedImageBackingFactory` supporting
`SHARED_IMAGE_USAGE_WEBNN_SHARED_TENSOR` (anything other than Windows
D3D or macOS IOSurface), `SharedImageStub::OnCreateSharedImage()` fails
and calls `OnError()` -> `channel_->Stop()`, tearing down the renderer's
`GpuChannel` and causing `ProduceWebNNTensor()` to fail with
`UnknownError` instead of `NotSupportedError` (or racing with subsequent
WebGPU initialization). Similarly, contexts with
`in_renderer_context_handle_` set do not have GPU-process
`SharedImageManager` interop, so allocating a `SharedImage` is wasted
work.

Reject `MLContext::createExportableTensor()` with `NotSupportedError`
before touching `SharedGpuContext` or `CreateSharedImageForMLTensor()`
on unsupported platforms (`!BUILDFLAG(IS_WIN) && !BUILDFLAG(IS_MAC)`) or
when `in_renderer_context_handle_` is set.

parent

[iOS][amsc] 4/ Hide pushed suggestions on tab and frame lifecycle events

iOS doesn't forward focus and navigation events to
BrowserAutofillManager, so suggestions pushed to
AutofillSuggestionControllerIOS would outlive their field.

At this CL, AutofillTabHelper hides pushed suggestions when the tab is
hidden, the main frame navigates cross-document, focus moves to another
field, or the frame holding the suggestions goes away. Hiding on blur is
deferred until the keyboard accessory is connected to the controller in
a follow-up CL.

This CL is part of a chain of CLs to rollout the new BAM suggestion
control flow, namely Autofill Manager Suggestion Control (AMSC).

Summary of the changes:
- AutofillSuggestionControllerIOS: add IsShowingForFrame.
- AutofillTabHelper: hide pushed suggestions on WasHidden,
  cross-document DidFinishNavigation, focus changes in
  FormActivityRegistered, and WebFrameBecameUnavailable.

[symphonia] Cover more audio formats in AudioPlaybackTest

AudioPlaybackTest runs each audio-only file on both FFmpegDemuxer and
SymphoniaDemuxer, but it has no Ogg or Opus files, only some WAV sample
formats, and no seeking. Those are all part of the audio container
launch for SymphoniaDemuxer.

WebM. Add AudioSeekTest, which seeks while playing on each demuxer.
These replace the tests in crrev.com/c/8641544.

tree
07ac232ae798dd3e046b44363be979e634bed74a
parent
c684e5686b9cc2bc88459499ca8f8deafe77529c [ diff ] 
[media] Add a FuzzTest for mp4::AAC::Parse()

mp4::AAC::Parse() decodes the AudioSpecificConfig of AAC streams, both
from the MP4 esds box (box_definitions.cc) and from FFmpeg extradata
(ffmpeg_common.cc), so its input comes from web content. No fuzz target
calls it directly today; the pipeline integration fuzzers reach it only
through whole MP4 streams.

Add a FUZZ_TEST next to the AAC unit tests that passes arbitrary bytes
to Parse() and then calls every accessor that reads the parsed state,
including CreateAdtsFromEsds(). It is seeded with valid configs for
AAC-LC, SSR, HE-AAC v1 and v2, the 24-bit sampling frequency escape and
xHE-AAC, plus an input too large for an ADTS header.

FuzzTest is the preferred framework for new fuzz targets
(testing/libfuzzer/README.md). In regular builds the test runs briefly
as part of media_unittests.

Bug: None
